# KTM 125 SX 2024: aftermarket parts

4840 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 470 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1806 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2564 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
